
============================================================
PRISMA MODEL: TimetableProject
============================================================
model TimetableProject {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdById String
  createdBy   User   @relation("TimetableProjectCreator", fields: [createdById], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  name         String
  academicYear String
  semester     String

  status TimetableProjectStatus @default(DRAFT)

  daysJson     Json
  periodsJson  Json
  settingsJson Json?

  teachers          TimetableTeacher[]
  classes           TimetableClass[]
  subjects          TimetableSubject[]
  classSubjects     TimetableClassSubject[]
  assignments       TimetableAssignment[]
  waitingPolicy     TimetableWaitingPolicy?
  dailyAbsences     TimetableDailyAbsence[]
  substitutions     TimetableSubstitution[]
  supervisionDuties TimetableSupervisionDuty[]
  constraints       TimetableConstraint[]

  schedules TimetableSchedule[]
  createdAt DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime            @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([createdById])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

============================================================
PRISMA MODEL: TimetableTeacher
============================================================
model TimetableTeacher {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  userId String?
  user   User?   @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  name          String
  specialty     String?
  maxWeeklyLoad Int     @default(24)
  isActive      Boolean @default(true)

  unavailableSlotsJson Json?

  assignments            TimetableAssignment[]
  dailyAbsences          TimetableDailyAbsence[]
  originalSubstitutions  TimetableSubstitution[]          @relation("OriginalSubstitutionTeacher")
  assignedSubstitutions  TimetableSubstitution[]          @relation("AssignedSubstitutionTeacher")
  supervisionAssignments TimetableSupervisionAssignment[]
  constraintLinks        TimetableConstraintTeacher[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([projectId, userId])
  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([userId])
  @@index([isActive])
}

PRISMA MODEL: TimetableClass

model TimetableClass {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  name     String
  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  subjects        TimetableClassSubject[]
  assignments     TimetableAssignment[]
  constraintLinks TimetableConstraintClass[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([projectId, name])
  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([isActive])
}

  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([catalogKey])
  @@index([isActive])

  @@index([projectId])

PRISMA MODEL: TimetableAssignment

model TimetableAssignment {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  teacherId String
  teacher   TimetableTeacher @relation(fields: [teacherId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  classId String
  class   TimetableClass @relation(fields: [classId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  subjectId String
  subject   TimetableSubject @relation(fields: [subjectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  assignedLessons Int
  singlePeriods   Int @default(0)
  doublePeriods   Int @default(0)

  fixedSlotsJson Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([projectId, teacherId, classId, subjectId])
  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([teacherId])
  @@index([classId])
  @@index([subjectId])
}

============================================================
PRISMA MODEL: TimetableConstraint
============================================================
model TimetableConstraint {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade, map: "TC_project_fk")

  type     String
  strength String @default("HARD")

  title    String?
  valueInt Int?
  notes    String? @db.Text

  configJson Json?

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  teachers TimetableConstraintTeacher[]
  subjects TimetableConstraintSubject[]
  classes  TimetableConstraintClass[]
  days     TimetableConstraintDay[]
  periods  TimetableConstraintPeriod[]
  slots    TimetableConstraintSlot[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([projectId], map: "TC_project_idx")
  @@index([projectId, type], map: "TC_project_type_idx")
  @@index([projectId, isActive], map: "TC_project_active_idx")
}

  @@unique([constraintId, teacherId], map: "TCT_constraint_teacher_key")
  @@index([teacherId], map: "TCT_teacher_idx")
}

PRISMA MODEL: TimetableSchedule
============================================================
model TimetableSchedule {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade, map: "TS_project_fk")

  version   Int
  status    String  @default("GENERATED")
  isCurrent Boolean @default(false)

  score          Int @default(0)
  completeness   Int @default(0)
  hardViolations Int @default(0)
  softPenalty    Int @default(0)

  attemptCount Int
  seed         Int
  durationMs   Int @default(0)

  engineVersion   String
  dataFingerprint String @db.VarChar(64)

  diagnosticsJson Json?
  configJson      Json?

  createdById String?

  entries TimetableScheduleEntry[]

  generatedAt DateTime @default(now())
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([projectId, version], map: "TS_project_version_key")
  @@index([projectId, isCurrent], map: "TS_project_current_idx")
  @@index([projectId, status], map: "TS_project_status_idx")
  @@index([generatedAt], map: "TS_generated_idx")
}

PRISMA MODEL: TimetableScheduleEntry
============================================================
model TimetableScheduleEntry {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  scheduleId String
  schedule   TimetableSchedule @relation(fields: [scheduleId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade, map: "TSE_schedule_fk")

  assignmentId String?

  teacherId   String
  teacherName String

  classId   String
  className String

  subjectId   String
  subjectName String

  dayId    String
  dayLabel String

  periodId    String
  periodLabel String
  periodOrder Int

  isLocked Boolean @default(false)
  source   String  @default("GENERATED")

  placementScore Int @default(0)

  metadataJson Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([scheduleId, classId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_schedule_class_slot_key")
  @@unique([scheduleId, teacherId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_schedule_teacher_slot_key")
  @@index([scheduleId], map: "TSE_schedule_idx")
  @@index([teacherId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_teacher_slot_idx")
  @@index([classId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_class_slot_idx")
  @@index([subjectId], map: "TSE_subject_idx")
  @@index([assignmentId], map: "TSE_assignment_idx")
}

PRISMA MODEL: TimetableWaitingPolicy
============================================================
model TimetableWaitingPolicy {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  projectId String           @unique
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  candidateCount Int @default(6)

  maxDailySubstitutions  Int @default(1)
  maxWeeklySubstitutions Int @default(5)

  allowBeforeFirstLesson Boolean @default(false)
  allowAfterLastLesson   Boolean @default(false)
  allowInsideGap         Boolean @default(true)

  preferInsideGap Boolean @default(true)

  allowOnGoldenDay   Boolean @default(false)
  goldenDayEmergency Boolean @default(false)

  allowAfterLateArrival Boolean @default(true)
  excludeLateArrivalDay Boolean @default(false)

  allowBeforeEarlyDeparture Boolean @default(true)

  preventConsecutiveSubstitutions Boolean @default(true)
  preventFirstPeriod              Boolean @default(false)
  preventLastPeriod               Boolean @default(false)

  requireMatchingSpecialty Boolean @default(false)
  preferMatchingSpecialty  Boolean @default(true)

  weeklyLoadWeight        Int @default(100)
  weeklyWaitingWeight     Int @default(40)
  dailyWaitingWeight      Int @default(60)
  gapPreferenceWeight     Int @default(20)
  specialtyWeight         Int @default(15)
  firstLastFairnessWeight Int @default(10)

  settingsJson Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([projectId])
}

model TimetableDailyAbsence {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  teacherId String
  teacher   TimetableTeacher @relation(fields: [teacherId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  absenceDate DateTime               @db.Date
  absenceType TimetableAbsenceType
  status      TimetableAbsenceStatus @default(ACTIVE)

  periodIdsJson Json?

  arrivalPeriodId   String?
  departurePeriodId String?

  reason String? @db.Text
  note   String? @db.Text

  createdById String?

  substitutions TimetableSubstitution[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([projectId, teacherId, absenceDate])
  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([teacherId])
  @@index([absenceDate])
  @@index([status])
}

PRISMA MODEL: TimetableSubstitution

model TimetableSubstitution {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  projectId String
  project   TimetableProject @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  absenceId String
  absence   TimetableDailyAbsence @relation(fields: [absenceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  substitutionDate DateTime @db.Date

  originalSessionId String
  dayId             String
  periodId          String

  classId     String
  className   String
  subjectId   String
  subjectName String

  originalTeacherId String
  originalTeacher   TimetableTeacher @relation("OriginalSubstitutionTeacher", fields: [originalTeacherId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  substituteTeacherId String?
  substituteTeacher   TimetableTeacher? @relation("AssignedSubstitutionTeacher", fields: [substituteTeacherId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  status TimetableSubstitutionStatus @default(PENDING)

  candidateRank  Int?
  candidateScore Int?

  candidatesJson  Json?
  selectionReason String? @db.Text
  overrideReason  String? @db.Text

  assignedAt  DateTime?
  notifiedAt  DateTime?
  completedAt DateTime?
  declinedAt  DateTime?
  canceledAt  DateTime?

  createdById String?
  updatedById String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([projectId, substitutionDate, originalSessionId], map: "TimetableSubstitution_project_date_session_key")
  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([absenceId])
  @@index([substitutionDate])
  @@index([originalTeacherId])
  @@index([substituteTeacherId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([dayId, periodId])
}

model TimetableSupervisionDuty {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  projectId String

  dutyType TimetableSupervisionType
  status   TimetableSupervisionStatus @default(DRAFT)

  dayId     String
  periodId  String?
  startTime String?
  endTime   String?

  location         String?
  requiredTeachers Int     @default(1)

  note String? @db.Text

  assignments TimetableSupervisionAssignment[]

  createdById String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([projectId])
  @@index([dayId])
  @@index([periodId])
  @@index([dutyType])
  @@index([status])
}

model TimetableSupervisionAssignment {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  dutyId String
  duty   TimetableSupervisionDuty @relation(fields: [dutyId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  teacherId String
  teacher   TimetableTeacher @relation(fields: [teacherId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  isPrimary Boolean @default(true)
  sortOrder Int     @default(0)

  assignedAt  DateTime  @default(now())
  completedAt DateTime?
  note        String?   @db.Text

  @@unique([dutyId, teacherId])
  @@index([dutyId])
  @@index([teacherId])
  @@index([isPrimary])
}

FILE: lib\timetable-v2\generation\generation-domain.ts
============================================================
export type GenerationDay = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  order: number;
};

export type GenerationPeriod = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  order: number;
};

export type GenerationTeacher = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  specialty: string | null;
  maxWeeklyLoad: number;
};

export type GenerationClass = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
};

export type GenerationSubject = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
};

export type GenerationAssignment = {
  id: string;

  teacherId: string;
  teacherName: string;

  classId: string;
  className: string;

  subjectId: string;
  subjectName: string;

  assignedLessons: number;

  singlePeriods: number;
  doublePeriods: number;
};

export type GenerationConstraint = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  strength: string;

  valueInt: number | null;
  weight: number;

  teacherIds: string[];
  subjectIds: string[];
  classIds: string[];

  dayIds: string[];
  periodIds: string[];

  slots: Array<{
    dayId: string;
    periodId: string;
  }>;
};

export type GenerationProblem = {
  projectId: string;

  days: GenerationDay[];
  periods: GenerationPeriod[];

  teachers: GenerationTeacher[];
  classes: GenerationClass[];
  subjects: GenerationSubject[];

  assignments: GenerationAssignment[];

  constraints: GenerationConstraint[];
};

export type GenerationTask = {
  id: string;
  assignmentId: string;

  teacherId: string;
  teacherName: string;

  classId: string;
  className: string;

  subjectId: string;
  subjectName: string;

  ordinal: number;
};

export type GeneratedSession = {
  temporaryId: string;

  assignmentId: string;

  teacherId: string;
  teacherName: string;

  classId: string;
  className: string;

  subjectId: string;
  subjectName: string;

  dayId: string;
  dayLabel: string;

  periodId: string;
  periodLabel: string;
  periodOrder: number;

  isLocked: boolean;

  source:
    | "GENERATED"
    | "FIXED_ASSIGNMENT"
    | "FIXED_TEACHER"
    | "FIXED_SUBJECT_DAY";

  placementScore: number;
};

export type GenerationDiagnostic = {
  code: string;
  level: "ERROR" | "WARNING" | "INFO";
  title: string;
  description: string;

  taskId?: string;
  assignmentId?: string;
  constraintId?: string;
};

export type GenerationAttemptResult = {
  complete: boolean;

  sessions: GeneratedSession[];

  requiredSessions: number;
  scheduledSessions: number;
  unscheduledSessions: number;

  completeness: number;

  softPenalty: number;
  score: number;

  diagnostics: GenerationDiagnostic[];
};

export type GenerationResult = {
  success: boolean;

  seed: number;
  attemptCount: number;
  completedAttempts: number;

  durationMs: number;

  best: GenerationAttemptResult;

  diagnostics: GenerationDiagnostic[];
};

export type GenerationOptions = {
  seed: number;
  attempts: number;
};

============================================================
FILE: lib\timetable-v2\generation\generation-engine.ts
============================================================
import type {
  GeneratedSession,
  GenerationAttemptResult,
  GenerationConstraint,
  GenerationDiagnostic,
  GenerationOptions,
  GenerationPeriod,
  GenerationProblem,
  GenerationResult,
  GenerationTask,
} from "./generation-domain";

const ENGINE_VERSION =
  "timetable-v2-engine-1.0.0";

export {
  ENGINE_VERSION,
};

type Slot = {
  dayId: string;
  periodId: string;
  periodOrder: number;
};

type EngineState = {
  sessions: GeneratedSession[];

  teacherBusy: Set<string>;
  classBusy: Set<string>;

  teacherDayCount:
    Map<string, number>;

  classDayCount:
    Map<string, number>;

  subjectClassDayCount:
    Map<string, number>;

  teacherDayPeriods:
    Map<string, Set<number>>;

  classDayPeriods:
    Map<string, Set<number>>;

  subjectClassDayPeriods:
    Map<string, Set<number>>;
};

type PreparedConstraint =
  GenerationConstraint & {
    effectiveSlots: Set<string>;
  };

function keySlot(
  dayId: string,
  periodId: string,
) {
  return `${dayId}:${periodId}`;
}

function keyTeacherSlot(
  teacherId: string,
  dayId: string,
  periodId: string,
) {
  return `${teacherId}:${dayId}:${periodId}`;
}

function keyClassSlot(
  classId: string,
  dayId: string,
  periodId: string,
) {
  return `${classId}:${dayId}:${periodId}`;
}

function keyTeacherDay(
  teacherId: string,
  dayId: string,
) {
  return `${teacherId}:${dayId}`;
}

function keyClassDay(
  classId: string,
  dayId: string,
) {
  return `${classId}:${dayId}`;
}

function keySubjectClassDay(
  classId: string,
  subjectId: string,
  dayId: string,
) {
  return `${classId}:${subjectId}:${dayId}`;
}

function createRandom(
  seed: number,
) {
  let value =
    seed >>> 0;

  return () => {
    value +=
      0x6d2b79f5;

    let t =
      value;

    t =
      Math.imul(
        t ^ (t >>> 15),
        t | 1,
      );

    t ^=
      t +
      Math.imul(
        t ^ (t >>> 7),
        t | 61,
      );

    return (
      (
        t ^
        (t >>> 14)
      ) >>> 0
    ) /
      4294967296;
  };
}

function numericWeight(
  constraint: GenerationConstraint,
) {
  if (
    Number.isFinite(
      constraint.weight,
    )
  ) {
    return Math.max(
      1,
      Math.min(
        100,
        Math.round(
          constraint.weight,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  return 1;
}

function appliesTarget(
  values: string[],
  value: string,
) {
  return (
    values.length === 0 ||
    values.includes(value)
  );
}

function targetsAssignment(
  constraint:
    GenerationConstraint,
  task:
    GenerationTask,
) {
  return (
    appliesTarget(
      constraint.teacherIds,
      task.teacherId,
    ) &&
    appliesTarget(
      constraint.subjectIds,
      task.subjectId,
    ) &&
    appliesTarget(
      constraint.classIds,
      task.classId,
    )
  );
}

function prepareConstraints(
  problem: GenerationProblem,
) {
  const dayIds =
    problem.days.map(
      (day) => day.id,
    );

  const periodIds =
    problem.periods.map(
      (period) =>
        period.id,
    );

  return problem.constraints.map(
    (
      constraint,
    ): PreparedConstraint => {
      const keys =
        new Set<string>();

      if (
        constraint.slots.length >
        0
      ) {
        for (
          const slot of
          constraint.slots
        ) {
          keys.add(
            keySlot(
              slot.dayId,
              slot.periodId,
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      else if (
        constraint.dayIds.length >
          0 &&
        constraint.periodIds.length >
          0
      ) {
        for (
          const dayId of
          constraint.dayIds
        ) {
          for (
            const periodId of
            constraint.periodIds
          ) {
            keys.add(
              keySlot(
                dayId,
                periodId,
              ),
            );
          }
        }
      }
      else if (
        constraint.dayIds.length >
        0
      ) {
        for (
          const dayId of
          constraint.dayIds
        ) {
          for (
            const periodId of
            periodIds
          ) {
            keys.add(
              keySlot(
                dayId,
                periodId,
              ),
            );
          }
        }
      }
      else if (
        constraint.periodIds.length >
        0
      ) {
        for (
          const dayId of
          dayIds
        ) {
          for (
            const periodId of
            constraint.periodIds
          ) {
            keys.add(
              keySlot(
                dayId,
                periodId,
              ),
            );
          }
        }
      }

      return {
        ...constraint,
        effectiveSlots:
          keys,
      };
    },
  );
}

function cloneSet(
  value?: Set<number>,
) {
  return new Set(
    value ?? [],
  );
}

function longestConsecutive(
  values: Set<number>,
) {
  const sorted =
    [...values].sort(
      (a, b) =>
        a - b,
    );

  let longest = 0;
  let current = 0;
  let previous:
    number | null =
      null;

  for (
    const value of sorted
  ) {
    if (
      previous !== null &&
      value ===
        previous + 1
    ) {
      current += 1;
    }
    else {
      current = 1;
    }

    longest =
      Math.max(
        longest,
        current,
      );

    previous =
      value;
  }

  return longest;
}

function addMap(
  map: Map<string, number>,
  key: string,
  amount = 1,
) {
  map.set(
    key,
    (
      map.get(key) ??
      0
    ) + amount,
  );
}

function addPeriod(
  map:
    Map<
      string,
      Set<number>
    >,
  key: string,
  periodOrder: number,
) {
  const next =
    cloneSet(
      map.get(key),
    );

  next.add(
    periodOrder,
  );

  map.set(
    key,
    next,
  );
}

function newState():
  EngineState {
  return {
    sessions: [],

    teacherBusy:
      new Set(),

    classBusy:
      new Set(),

    teacherDayCount:
      new Map(),

    classDayCount:
      new Map(),

    subjectClassDayCount:
      new Map(),

    teacherDayPeriods:
      new Map(),

    classDayPeriods:
      new Map(),

    subjectClassDayPeriods:
      new Map(),
  };
}

function slotIsSpecified(
  constraint:
    PreparedConstraint,
) {
  return (
    constraint.effectiveSlots
      .size > 0
  );
}

function slotMatches(
  constraint:
    PreparedConstraint,
  slot: Slot,
) {
  if (
    !slotIsSpecified(
      constraint,
    )
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  return constraint.effectiveSlots.has(
    keySlot(
      slot.dayId,
      slot.periodId,
    ),
  );
}

function isHard(
  constraint:
    PreparedConstraint,
) {
  return (
    constraint.strength ===
    "HARD"
  );
}

function currentCount(
  map: Map<string, number>,
  key: string,
) {
  return (
    map.get(key) ??
    0
  );
}

function wouldExceedConsecutive(
  map:
    Map<
      string,
      Set<number>
    >,
  key: string,
  periodOrder: number,
  max: number,
) {
  const next =
    cloneSet(
      map.get(key),
    );

  next.add(
    periodOrder,
  );

  return (
    longestConsecutive(
      next,
    ) > max
  );
}

function hasAdjacentSameSubject(
  state: EngineState,
  task: GenerationTask,
  dayId: string,
  periodOrder: number,
) {
  const periods =
    state.subjectClassDayPeriods.get(
      keySubjectClassDay(
        task.classId,
        task.subjectId,
        dayId,
      ),
    );

  if (!periods) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    periods.has(
      periodOrder - 1,
    ) ||
    periods.has(
      periodOrder + 1,
    )
  );
}

function hardAllows(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  task:
    GenerationTask,
  slot:
    Slot,
) {
  if (
    state.teacherBusy.has(
      keyTeacherSlot(
        task.teacherId,
        slot.dayId,
        slot.periodId,
      ),
    )
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    state.classBusy.has(
      keyClassSlot(
        task.classId,
        slot.dayId,
        slot.periodId,
      ),
    )
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const firstOrder =
    problem.periods[0]
      ?.order;

  const lastOrder =
    problem.periods[
      problem.periods.length -
        1
    ]?.order;

  for (
    const constraint of
    prepared
  ) {
    if (
      !isHard(
        constraint,
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const type =
      constraint.type;

    const matches =
      targetsAssignment(
        constraint,
        task,
      );

    const inSlot =
      slotMatches(
        constraint,
        slot,
      );

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "SCHOOL_BLOCKED_SLOT" ||
        type ===
          "SCHOOL_BLOCKED_DAY" ||
        type ===
          "SCHOOL_NO_TEACHING_SLOT"
      ) &&
      inSlot
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "TEACHER_UNAVAILABLE" ||
        type ===
          "TEACHER_DAY_OFF"
      ) &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      inSlot
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_BLOCKED" ||
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_BLOCKED_DAYS"
      ) &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      inSlot
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_ALLOWED_DAYS" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      constraint.dayIds.length >
        0 &&
      !constraint.dayIds.includes(
        slot.dayId,
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_ALLOWED_PERIODS" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      constraint.periodIds.length >
        0 &&
      !constraint.periodIds.includes(
        slot.periodId,
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "CLASS_BLOCKED_SLOT" ||
        type ===
          "CLASS_BLOCKED_DAY" ||
        type ===
          "CLASS_BLOCKED_PERIOD"
      ) &&
      constraint.classIds.includes(
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      inSlot
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "ASSIGNMENT_BLOCKED_SLOT" &&
      matches &&
      inSlot
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_SPECIFIC_TEACHER" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      constraint.teacherIds.length >
        0 &&
      !constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "TEACHER_MAX_DAILY" ||
        type ===
          "TEACHER_DAILY_LIMIT"
      ) &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      currentCount(
        state.teacherDayCount,
        keyTeacherDay(
          task.teacherId,
          slot.dayId,
        ),
      ) + 1 >
        constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_MAX_CONSECUTIVE" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      wouldExceedConsecutive(
        state.teacherDayPeriods,
        keyTeacherDay(
          task.teacherId,
          slot.dayId,
        ),
        slot.periodOrder,
        constraint.valueInt,
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "CLASS_DAILY_LIMIT" ||
        type ===
          "CLASS_MAX_DAILY"
      ) &&
      constraint.classIds.includes(
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      currentCount(
        state.classDayCount,
        keyClassDay(
          task.classId,
          slot.dayId,
        ),
      ) + 1 >
        constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "CLASS_CONSECUTIVE_LIMIT" ||
        type ===
          "CLASS_MAX_CONSECUTIVE"
      ) &&
      constraint.classIds.includes(
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      wouldExceedConsecutive(
        state.classDayPeriods,
        keyClassDay(
          task.classId,
          slot.dayId,
        ),
        slot.periodOrder,
        constraint.valueInt,
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_DAILY_LIMIT" ||
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_MAX_DAILY"
      ) &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      currentCount(
        state.subjectClassDayCount,
        keySubjectClassDay(
          task.classId,
          task.subjectId,
          slot.dayId,
        ),
      ) + 1 >
        constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "CLASS_NO_DOUBLE" ||
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_NO_CONSECUTIVE"
      ) &&
      matches &&
      hasAdjacentSameSubject(
        state,
        task,
        slot.dayId,
        slot.periodOrder,
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_AVOID_BEFORE_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      slot.periodOrder <
        constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_AVOID_AFTER_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt &&
      slot.periodOrder >
        constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_AVOID_FIRST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        firstOrder
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_AVOID_LAST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        lastOrder
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_AVOID_FIRST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        firstOrder
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_AVOID_LAST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        lastOrder
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

function softPenaltyForCandidate(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  task:
    GenerationTask,
  slot:
    Slot,
) {
  let penalty = 0;

  const firstOrder =
    problem.periods[0]
      ?.order;

  const lastOrder =
    problem.periods[
      problem.periods.length -
        1
    ]?.order;

  for (
    const constraint of
    prepared
  ) {
    if (
      constraint.strength ===
      "HARD"
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const type =
      constraint.type;

    const weight =
      numericWeight(
        constraint,
      );

    const matches =
      targetsAssignment(
        constraint,
        task,
      );

    const inSlot =
      slotMatches(
        constraint,
        slot,
      );

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_PREFERRED" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      !inSlot
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_PREFERRED" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      appliesTarget(
        constraint.classIds,
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      !inSlot
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "CLASS_PREFERRED_SLOT" ||
        type ===
          "CLASS_PREFERRED"
      ) &&
      constraint.classIds.includes(
        task.classId,
      ) &&
      !inSlot
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "PREFERRED_FIRST_PERIODS" ||
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_EARLY_PERIODS"
      ) &&
      (
        constraint.subjectIds.length ===
          0 ||
        constraint.subjectIds.includes(
          task.subjectId,
        )
      ) &&
      constraint.periodIds.length >
        0 &&
      !constraint.periodIds.includes(
        slot.periodId,
      )
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_LATE_PERIODS" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      constraint.periodIds.length >
        0 &&
      !constraint.periodIds.includes(
        slot.periodId,
      )
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "EVEN_DISTRIBUTION" ||
        type ===
          "FAIR_SUBJECT_SPREAD" ||
        type ===
          "SUBJECT_DAILY_SEPARATION"
      ) &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      )
    ) {
      penalty +=
        currentCount(
          state.subjectClassDayCount,
          keySubjectClassDay(
            task.classId,
            task.subjectId,
            slot.dayId,
          ),
        ) *
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.round(
            weight / 5,
          ),
        );
    }

    if (
      (
        type ===
          "TEACHER_MAX_DAILY" ||
        type ===
          "TEACHER_DAILY_LIMIT"
      ) &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      const next =
        currentCount(
          state.teacherDayCount,
          keyTeacherDay(
            task.teacherId,
            slot.dayId,
          ),
        ) + 1;

      if (
        next >
        constraint.valueInt
      ) {
        penalty +=
          (
            next -
            constraint.valueInt
          ) *
          weight;
      }
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_MAX_CONSECUTIVE" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      constraint.valueInt
    ) {
      const next =
        cloneSet(
          state.teacherDayPeriods.get(
            keyTeacherDay(
              task.teacherId,
              slot.dayId,
            ),
          ),
        );

      next.add(
        slot.periodOrder,
      );

      const overflow =
        longestConsecutive(
          next,
        ) -
        constraint.valueInt;

      if (
        overflow > 0
      ) {
        penalty +=
          overflow *
          weight;
      }
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "NO_ISOLATED_PERIOD" &&
      matches &&
      !hasAdjacentSameSubject(
        state,
        task,
        slot.dayId,
        slot.periodOrder,
      )
    ) {
      penalty +=
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.round(
            weight / 4,
          ),
        );
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_PREFER_CONSECUTIVE" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      !hasAdjacentSameSubject(
        state,
        task,
        slot.dayId,
        slot.periodOrder,
      )
    ) {
      penalty +=
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.round(
            weight / 3,
          ),
        );
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_AVOID_FIRST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        firstOrder
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "TEACHER_AVOID_LAST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.teacherIds.includes(
        task.teacherId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        lastOrder
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_AVOID_FIRST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        firstOrder
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }

    if (
      type ===
        "SUBJECT_AVOID_LAST_PERIOD" &&
      constraint.subjectIds.includes(
        task.subjectId,
      ) &&
      slot.periodOrder ===
        lastOrder
    ) {
      penalty +=
        weight;
    }
  }

  const teacherDayCount =
    currentCount(
      state.teacherDayCount,
      keyTeacherDay(
        task.teacherId,
        slot.dayId,
      ),
    );

  penalty +=
    teacherDayCount;

  const sameSubjectToday =
    currentCount(
      state.subjectClassDayCount,
      keySubjectClassDay(
        task.classId,
        task.subjectId,
        slot.dayId,
      ),
    );

  penalty +=
    sameSubjectToday * 2;

  return penalty;
}

function place(
  state:
    EngineState,
  task:
    GenerationTask,
  day:
    GenerationProblem["days"][number],
  period:
    GenerationPeriod,
  source:
    GeneratedSession["source"],
  isLocked:
    boolean,
  placementScore:
    number,
) {
  state.teacherBusy.add(
    keyTeacherSlot(
      task.teacherId,
      day.id,
      period.id,
    ),
  );

  state.classBusy.add(
    keyClassSlot(
      task.classId,
      day.id,
      period.id,
    ),
  );

  addMap(
    state.teacherDayCount,
    keyTeacherDay(
      task.teacherId,
      day.id,
    ),
  );

  addMap(
    state.classDayCount,
    keyClassDay(
      task.classId,
      day.id,
    ),
  );

  addMap(
    state.subjectClassDayCount,
    keySubjectClassDay(
      task.classId,
      task.subjectId,
      day.id,
    ),
  );

  addPeriod(
    state.teacherDayPeriods,
    keyTeacherDay(
      task.teacherId,
      day.id,
    ),
    period.order,
  );

  addPeriod(
    state.classDayPeriods,
    keyClassDay(
      task.classId,
      day.id,
    ),
    period.order,
  );

  addPeriod(
    state.subjectClassDayPeriods,
    keySubjectClassDay(
      task.classId,
      task.subjectId,
      day.id,
    ),
    period.order,
  );

  state.sessions.push({
    temporaryId:
      `${task.id}:${day.id}:${period.id}`,

    assignmentId:
      task.assignmentId,

    teacherId:
      task.teacherId,

    teacherName:
      task.teacherName,

    classId:
      task.classId,

    className:
      task.className,

    subjectId:
      task.subjectId,

    subjectName:
      task.subjectName,

    dayId:
      day.id,

    dayLabel:
      day.label,

    periodId:
      period.id,

    periodLabel:
      period.label,

    periodOrder:
      period.order,

    isLocked,

    source,

    placementScore,
  });
}

function makeTasks(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
) {
  const tasks:
    GenerationTask[] =
      [];

  for (
    const assignment of
    problem.assignments
  ) {
    for (
      let ordinal = 1;
      ordinal <=
        assignment.assignedLessons;
      ordinal += 1
    ) {
      tasks.push({
        id:
          `${assignment.id}:${ordinal}`,

        assignmentId:
          assignment.id,

        teacherId:
          assignment.teacherId,

        teacherName:
          assignment.teacherName,

        classId:
          assignment.classId,

        className:
          assignment.className,

        subjectId:
          assignment.subjectId,

        subjectName:
          assignment.subjectName,

        ordinal,
      });
    }
  }

  return tasks;
}

function findTask(
  remaining:
    GenerationTask[],
  constraint:
    PreparedConstraint,
) {
  return remaining.find(
    (task) =>
      targetsAssignment(
        constraint,
        task,
      ),
  );
}

function removeTask(
  remaining:
    GenerationTask[],
  taskId:
    string,
) {
  const index =
    remaining.findIndex(
      (task) =>
        task.id ===
        taskId,
    );

  if (
    index >= 0
  ) {
    remaining.splice(
      index,
      1,
    );
  }
}

function findDay(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  dayId:
    string,
) {
  return problem.days.find(
    (day) =>
      day.id ===
      dayId,
  );
}

function findPeriod(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  periodId:
    string,
) {
  return problem.periods.find(
    (period) =>
      period.id ===
      periodId,
  );
}

function preplaceFixedAssignments(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  remaining:
    GenerationTask[],
  diagnostics:
    GenerationDiagnostic[],
) {
  const fixed =
    prepared.filter(
      (constraint) =>
        constraint.strength ===
          "HARD" &&
        constraint.type ===
          "FIXED_ASSIGNMENT",
    );

  for (
    const constraint of
    fixed
  ) {
    const slots =
      [...constraint.effectiveSlots];

    for (
      const slotKeyValue of
      slots
    ) {
      const [
        dayId,
        periodId,
      ] =
        slotKeyValue.split(":");

      const day =
        findDay(
          problem,
          dayId,
        );

      const period =
        findPeriod(
          problem,
          periodId,
        );

      if (
        !day ||
        !period
      ) {
        diagnostics.push({
          code:
            "FIXED_SLOT_INVALID",

          level:
            "ERROR",

          title:
            "تثبيت يشير إلى وقت غير صالح",

          description:
            "أحد قيود التثبيت يشير إلى يوم أو حصة غير موجودة.",

          constraintId:
            constraint.id,
        });

        return false;
      }

      const task =
        findTask(
          remaining,
          constraint,
        );

      if (!task) {
        diagnostics.push({
          code:
            "FIXED_ASSIGNMENT_NO_LESSON",

          level:
            "ERROR",

          title:
            "عدد التثبيتات أكبر من الإسناد",

          description:
            "لا توجد حصة متبقية مطابقة لهذا التثبيت.",

          constraintId:
            constraint.id,
        });

        return false;
      }

      const slot = {
        dayId:
          day.id,

        periodId:
          period.id,

        periodOrder:
          period.order,
      };

      if (
        !hardAllows(
          problem,
          prepared,
          state,
          task,
          slot,
        )
      ) {
        diagnostics.push({
          code:
            "FIXED_ASSIGNMENT_CONFLICT",

          level:
            "ERROR",

          title:
            "تعذر تطبيق تثبيت إلزامي",

          description:
            `${task.teacherName} / ${task.subjectName} / ${task.className} يتعارض مع قيد إلزامي أو إشغال موجود.`,

          taskId:
            task.id,

          assignmentId:
            task.assignmentId,

          constraintId:
            constraint.id,
        });

        return false;
      }

      place(
        state,
        task,
        day,
        period,
        "FIXED_ASSIGNMENT",
        true,
        0,
      );

      removeTask(
        remaining,
        task.id,
      );
    }
  }

  return true;
}

function preplaceFixedTeachers(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  remaining:
    GenerationTask[],
  diagnostics:
    GenerationDiagnostic[],
) {
  const rules =
    prepared.filter(
      (constraint) =>
        constraint.strength ===
          "HARD" &&
        constraint.type ===
          "FIXED_TEACHER_SLOT",
    );

  for (
    const constraint of
    rules
  ) {
    for (
      const value of
      constraint.effectiveSlots
    ) {
      const [
        dayId,
        periodId,
      ] =
        value.split(":");

      const day =
        findDay(
          problem,
          dayId,
        );

      const period =
        findPeriod(
          problem,
          periodId,
        );

      if (
        !day ||
        !period
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      const candidates =
        remaining.filter(
          (task) =>
            constraint.teacherIds.includes(
              task.teacherId,
            ),
        );

      let placed =
        false;

      for (
        const task of
        candidates
      ) {
        const slot = {
          dayId,
          periodId,
          periodOrder:
            period.order,
        };

        if (
          !hardAllows(
            problem,
            prepared,
            state,
            task,
            slot,
          )
        ) {
          continue;
        }

        place(
          state,
          task,
          day,
          period,
          "FIXED_TEACHER",
          true,
          0,
        );

        removeTask(
          remaining,
          task.id,
        );

        placed =
          true;

        break;
      }

      if (!placed) {
        diagnostics.push({
          code:
            "FIXED_TEACHER_SLOT_UNSATISFIED",

          level:
            "ERROR",

          title:
            "تعذر تثبيت المعلم",

          description:
            "لا توجد حصة متبقية قابلة للوضع للمعلم في الخلية المثبتة.",

          constraintId:
            constraint.id,
        });

        return false;
      }
    }
  }

  return true;
}

function preplaceFixedSubjectDays(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  remaining:
    GenerationTask[],
  diagnostics:
    GenerationDiagnostic[],
  random:
    () => number,
) {
  const rules =
    prepared.filter(
      (constraint) =>
        constraint.strength ===
          "HARD" &&
        constraint.type ===
          "FIXED_SUBJECT_DAY",
    );

  for (
    const constraint of
    rules
  ) {
    for (
      const dayId of
      constraint.dayIds
    ) {
      const day =
        findDay(
          problem,
          dayId,
        );

      if (!day) {
        continue;
      }

      const tasks =
        remaining
          .filter(
            (task) =>
              targetsAssignment(
                constraint,
                task,
              ),
          )
          .sort(
            () =>
              random() -
              0.5,
          );

      let placed =
        false;

      for (
        const task of
        tasks
      ) {
        const periods =
          [...problem.periods]
            .sort(
              () =>
                random() -
                0.5,
            );

        for (
          const period of
          periods
        ) {
          const slot = {
            dayId:
              day.id,

            periodId:
              period.id,

            periodOrder:
              period.order,
          };

          if (
            !hardAllows(
              problem,
              prepared,
              state,
              task,
              slot,
            )
          ) {
            continue;
          }

          place(
            state,
            task,
            day,
            period,
            "FIXED_SUBJECT_DAY",
            true,
            0,
          );

          removeTask(
            remaining,
            task.id,
          );

          placed =
            true;

          break;
        }

        if (placed) {
          break;
        }
      }

      if (!placed) {
        diagnostics.push({
          code:
            "FIXED_SUBJECT_DAY_UNSATISFIED",

          level:
            "ERROR",

          title:
            "تعذر تثبيت المادة في اليوم",

          description:
            "لم يجد المحرك حصة قابلة للتثبيت لهذه المادة في اليوم المحدد.",

          constraintId:
            constraint.id,
        });

        return false;
      }
    }
  }

  return true;
}

function getCandidates(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  task:
    GenerationTask,
  random:
    () => number,
) {
  const candidates:
    Array<{
      day:
        GenerationProblem["days"][number];

      period:
        GenerationPeriod;

      penalty:
        number;

      random:
        number;
    }> = [];

  for (
    const day of
    problem.days
  ) {
    for (
      const period of
      problem.periods
    ) {
      const slot = {
        dayId:
          day.id,

        periodId:
          period.id,

        periodOrder:
          period.order,
      };

      if (
        !hardAllows(
          problem,
          prepared,
          state,
          task,
          slot,
        )
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      candidates.push({
        day,
        period,

        penalty:
          softPenaltyForCandidate(
            problem,
            prepared,
            state,
            task,
            slot,
          ),

        random:
          random(),
      });
    }
  }

  candidates.sort(
    (a, b) =>
      a.penalty -
        b.penalty ||
      a.random -
        b.random,
  );

  return candidates;
}

function calculateGapCount(
  periods:
    Set<number>,
) {
  if (
    periods.size < 2
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  const sorted =
    [...periods].sort(
      (a, b) =>
        a - b,
    );

  const first =
    sorted[0];

  const last =
    sorted[
      sorted.length - 1
    ];

  return Math.max(
    0,
    last -
      first +
      1 -
      sorted.length,
  );
}

function variance(
  values: number[],
) {
  if (
    values.length === 0
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  const average =
    values.reduce(
      (sum, value) =>
        sum + value,
      0,
    ) /
    values.length;

  return (
    values.reduce(
      (
        sum,
        value,
      ) =>
        sum +
        Math.pow(
          value -
            average,
          2,
        ),
      0,
    ) /
    values.length
  );
}

function finalSoftPenalty(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
) {
  let penalty = 0;

  for (
    const periods of
    state.teacherDayPeriods.values()
  ) {
    penalty +=
      calculateGapCount(
        periods,
      ) * 3;
  }

  for (
    const periods of
    state.classDayPeriods.values()
  ) {
    penalty +=
      calculateGapCount(
        periods,
      );
  }

  const firstOrder =
    problem.periods[0]
      ?.order;

  const lastOrder =
    problem.periods[
      problem.periods.length -
        1
    ]?.order;

  if (
    firstOrder !==
      undefined &&
    lastOrder !==
      undefined
  ) {
    const firstCounts =
      problem.teachers.map(
        (teacher) =>
          state.sessions.filter(
            (session) =>
              session.teacherId ===
                teacher.id &&
              session.periodOrder ===
                firstOrder,
          ).length,
      );

    const lastCounts =
      problem.teachers.map(
        (teacher) =>
          state.sessions.filter(
            (session) =>
              session.teacherId ===
                teacher.id &&
              session.periodOrder ===
                lastOrder,
          ).length,
      );

    penalty +=
      Math.round(
        variance(
          firstCounts,
        ) * 2,
      );

    penalty +=
      Math.round(
        variance(
          lastCounts,
        ) * 2,
      );
  }

  for (
    const constraint of
    prepared
  ) {
    if (
      constraint.strength ===
      "HARD"
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const weight =
      numericWeight(
        constraint,
      );

    if (
      constraint.type ===
        "TEACHER_MIN_DAILY" &&
      constraint.valueInt !==
        null
    ) {
      for (
        const teacherId of
        constraint.teacherIds
      ) {
        for (
          const day of
          problem.days
        ) {
          const count =
            currentCount(
              state.teacherDayCount,
              keyTeacherDay(
                teacherId,
                day.id,
              ),
            );

          if (
            count > 0 &&
            count <
              constraint.valueInt
          ) {
            penalty +=
              (
                constraint.valueInt -
                count
              ) *
              weight;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (
      (
        constraint.type ===
          "FAIR_TEACHER_GAPS" ||
        constraint.type ===
          "TEACHER_COMPACT_DAY"
      )
    ) {
      let gaps = 0;

      for (
        const [
          key,
          periods,
        ] of
        state.teacherDayPeriods
      ) {
        if (
          constraint.teacherIds.length >
            0 &&
          !constraint.teacherIds.some(
            (teacherId) =>
              key.startsWith(
                `${teacherId}:`,
              ),
          )
        ) {
          continue;
        }

        gaps +=
          calculateGapCount(
            periods,
          );
      }

      penalty +=
        gaps *
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.round(
            weight / 5,
          ),
        );
    }
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.round(
      penalty,
    ),
  );
}

function attempt(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  seed:
    number,
) {
  const random =
    createRandom(seed);

  const state =
    newState();

  const diagnostics:
    GenerationDiagnostic[] =
      [];

  const remaining =
    makeTasks(
      problem,
    );

  const requiredSessions =
    remaining.length;

  if (
    !preplaceFixedAssignments(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
      remaining,
      diagnostics,
    )
  ) {
    return finalizeAttempt(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
      requiredSessions,
      diagnostics,
    );
  }

  if (
    !preplaceFixedTeachers(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
      remaining,
      diagnostics,
    )
  ) {
    return finalizeAttempt(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
      requiredSessions,
      diagnostics,
    );
  }

  if (
    !preplaceFixedSubjectDays(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
      remaining,
      diagnostics,
      random,
    )
  ) {
    return finalizeAttempt(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
      requiredSessions,
      diagnostics,
    );
  }

  while (
    remaining.length >
    0
  ) {
    const sample =
      remaining
        .slice(
          0,
          Math.min(
            16,
            remaining.length,
          ),
        )
        .map(
          (task) => ({
            task,

            candidates:
              getCandidates(
                problem,
                prepared,
                state,
                task,
                random,
              ),
          }),
        )
        .sort(
          (a, b) =>
            a.candidates.length -
              b.candidates.length ||
            random() -
              0.5,
        );

    const choice =
      sample[0];

    if (
      !choice ||
      choice.candidates.length ===
        0
    ) {
      const task =
        choice?.task ??
        remaining[0];

      diagnostics.push({
        code:
          "NO_VALID_SLOT",

        level:
          "ERROR",

        title:
          "تعذر إيجاد خانة صالحة",

        description:
          `${task.teacherName} — ${task.subjectName} — ${task.className}: جميع الخانات المتبقية محجوبة أو مشغولة.`,

        taskId:
          task.id,

        assignmentId:
          task.assignmentId,
      });

      break;
    }

    const topCount =
      Math.min(
        4,
        choice.candidates.length,
      );

    const index =
      Math.floor(
        random() *
        topCount,
      );

    const candidate =
      choice.candidates[
        index
      ];

    place(
      state,
      choice.task,
      candidate.day,
      candidate.period,
      "GENERATED",
      false,
      candidate.penalty,
    );

    removeTask(
      remaining,
      choice.task.id,
    );
  }

  return finalizeAttempt(
    problem,
    prepared,
    state,
    requiredSessions,
    diagnostics,
  );
}

function finalizeAttempt(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  prepared:
    PreparedConstraint[],
  state:
    EngineState,
  requiredSessions:
    number,
  diagnostics:
    GenerationDiagnostic[],
): GenerationAttemptResult {
  const scheduledSessions =
    state.sessions.length;

  const unscheduledSessions =
    Math.max(
      0,
      requiredSessions -
        scheduledSessions,
    );

  const completeness =
    requiredSessions === 0
      ? 100
      : Math.round(
          (
            scheduledSessions /
            requiredSessions
          ) *
            100,
        );

  const softPenalty =
    finalSoftPenalty(
      problem,
      prepared,
      state,
    ) +
    state.sessions.reduce(
      (
        sum,
        session,
      ) =>
        sum +
        session.placementScore,
      0,
    );

  const normalizedPenalty =
    requiredSessions > 0
      ? softPenalty /
        requiredSessions
      : 0;

  const score =
    Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(
        100,
        Math.round(
          100 -
            normalizedPenalty *
              2,
        ),
      ),
    );

  return {
    complete:
      unscheduledSessions ===
      0,

    sessions:
      state.sessions,

    requiredSessions,
    scheduledSessions,
    unscheduledSessions,

    completeness,

    softPenalty,

    score,

    diagnostics,
  };
}

function betterThan(
  candidate:
    GenerationAttemptResult,
  current:
    GenerationAttemptResult | null,
) {
  if (!current) {
    return true;
  }

  if (
    candidate.complete !==
    current.complete
  ) {
    return candidate.complete;
  }

  if (
    candidate.scheduledSessions !==
    current.scheduledSessions
  ) {
    return (
      candidate.scheduledSessions >
      current.scheduledSessions
    );
  }

  if (
    candidate.score !==
    current.score
  ) {
    return (
      candidate.score >
      current.score
    );
  }

  return (
    candidate.softPenalty <
    current.softPenalty
  );
}

export function generateTimetableV2(
  problem:
    GenerationProblem,
  options:
    GenerationOptions,
): GenerationResult {
  const startedAt =
    Date.now();

  const attempts =
    Math.max(
      1,
      Math.min(
        60,
        Math.floor(
          options.attempts,
        ),
      ),
    );

  const prepared =
    prepareConstraints(
      problem,
    );

  let best:
    GenerationAttemptResult | null =
      null;

  let completedAttempts =
    0;

  const diagnostics:
    GenerationDiagnostic[] =
      [];

  for (
    let index = 0;
    index < attempts;
    index += 1
  ) {
    const attemptSeed =
      (
        options.seed +
        index *
          104729
      ) >>> 0;

    const result =
      attempt(
        problem,
        prepared,
        attemptSeed,
      );

    if (
      result.complete
    ) {
      completedAttempts +=
        1;
    }

    if (
      betterThan(
        result,
        best,
      )
    ) {
      best =
        result;
    }

    if (
      result.complete &&
      result.score >= 99
    ) {
      break;
    }
  }

  const finalBest =
    best ??
    {
      complete: false,
      sessions: [],
      requiredSessions: 0,
      scheduledSessions: 0,
      unscheduledSessions: 0,
      completeness: 0,
      softPenalty: 0,
      score: 0,
      diagnostics: [],
    };

  if (
    !finalBest.complete
  ) {
    diagnostics.push({
      code:
        "GENERATION_NOT_COMPLETE",

      level:
        "ERROR",

      title:
        "لم يكتمل الجدول",

      description:
        `أفضل محاولة وزعت ${finalBest.scheduledSessions} من ${finalBest.requiredSessions} حصة. راجع القيود والإسنادات الصعبة.`,
    });
  }

  return {
    success:
      finalBest.complete,

    seed:
      options.seed,

    attemptCount:
      attempts,

    completedAttempts,

    durationMs:
      Date.now() -
      startedAt,

    best:
      finalBest,

    diagnostics: [
      ...diagnostics,
      ...finalBest.diagnostics.slice(
        0,
        30,
      ),
    ],
  };
}
